      "content": "---\ndescription: 生成学术论文级 matplotlib 图表——按 narrative_intent + design_notes 设计，从 pattern 库选用技法，远程 SSH 执行输出 PDF/PNG\nname: draw-paper-figure\nscope: global\ntags:\n  - research\n  - visualization\n  - paper\n  - matplotlib\nversion: 2\ntools:\n  - ssh_execute\n---\n\n# Draw Paper Figure — 学术统计图生成（Worker 执行）\n\nWorker 收到统计图画图任务时读本 skill。**画图前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的 L1/L2 原则和 L3 pattern 库。\n\n**质量底线：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感（灰背景、粗网格线、tab10 色板、默认蓝色）。**\n\n**生成图片后必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮（Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）。第一版画出来基本不能直接用。**\n\n本 skill 提供：\n- 通用样式（rcParams + 色板）\n- 三个常用图表的起点模板（reliability/heatmap/scatter）\n- NeurIPS 经验提炼的 pattern 实现示例\n\n## 输入字段（从 Paper Agent 传入）\n\n- `fig_id` / `type` / `placement` / `data_source` / `aspect_ratio` / 输出路径\n- **`narrative_intent`**：一句话，这张图要让读者相信什么\n- **`design_notes`**：从 paper-visualization L3 选的 pattern 列表\n\n## 画图工作流\n\n1. 读 paper-visualization skill 的 L1 防丑原则 + L2 叙事原则\n2. 根据 `narrative_intent` 选起点模板（本 skill §模板）\n3. 按 `design_notes` 中每个 pattern 添加相应技法（本 skill §pattern 实现）\n4. 提交前过本 skill §checklist\n\n## 通用样式标准（所有图表必须遵守）\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nimport numpy as np\n\n# 字体和全局样式\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\n# 色盲友好配色：优先从 writing_plan.yaml 的 color_palette 读取\n# 读不到则 fallback 到以下默认值\nCOLORS = {\n    'fp16':   '#E69F00',  # 橙黄\n    'nf4':    '#56B4E9',  # 天蓝\n    'int8':   '#009E73',  # 绿\n    'gptq':   '#F0E442',  # 黄\n    'awq':    '#CC79A7',  # 粉紫\n    'llama':  '#0072B2',  # 深蓝\n    'qwen3':  '#D55E00',  # 橙红\n    'gemma':  '#009E73',  # 绿\n    'mistral':'#CC79A7',  # 粉紫\n    'phi3':   '#F0E442',  # 黄\n}\n\n# 方法标签\nMETHOD_LABELS = {\n    'fp16': 'FP16', 'nf4': 'BNB-NF4',\n    'int8': 'BNB-INT8', 'gptq': 'GPTQ-4bit', 'awq': 'AWQ-4bit'\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 1. Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n\n**用途**：显示模型 confidence bin vs accuracy，对角线 = 完美校准。\n\n```python\ndef plot_reliability_diagram(confidences, accuracies, n_bins=10,\n                              label='', color='#0072B2',\n                              title='Reliability Diagram',\n                              outpath='reliability.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    confidences, accuracies: 各样本的 confidence 和 binary correct (0/1)\n    \"\"\"\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n    bin_accs, bin_confs, bin_counts = [], [], []\n\n    for i in range(n_bins):\n        lo, hi = bin_edges[i], bin_edges[i+1]\n        mask = (confidences >= lo) & (confidences < hi)\n        if mask.sum() > 0:\n            bin_accs.append(accuracies[mask].mean())\n            bin_confs.append(confidences[mask].mean())\n            bin_counts.append(mask.sum())\n        else:\n            bin_accs.append(float('nan'))\n            bin_confs.append((lo + hi) / 2)\n            bin_counts.append(0)\n\n    bin_accs = np.array(bin_accs)\n    bin_confs = np.array(bin_confs)\n\n    fig, (ax_main, ax_hist) = plt.subplots(\n        2, 1, figsize=(4, 4.5), gridspec_kw={'height_ratios': [3, 1]},\n        sharex=True\n    )\n\n    # 主图：gaps\n    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                color=color, alpha=0.7, label='Accuracy', zorder=2)\n    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_confs - bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                bottom=bin_accs, color='red', alpha=0.3, label='Gap', zorder=2)\n    ax_main.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k--', linewidth=1, zorder=3, label='Perfect')\n    ax_main.set_xlim(0, 1); ax_main.set_ylim(0, 1)\n    ax_main.set_ylabel('Accuracy')\n    ax_main.set_title(title)\n    ax_main.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9)\n    ax_main.grid(True, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\n\n    # ECE annotation\n    ece = np.nansum(np.array(bin_counts) / sum(bin_counts) *\n                    np.abs(bin_accs - bin_confs))\n    ax_main.text(0.98, 0.02, f'ECE={ece:.3f}', transform=ax_main.transAxes,\n                 ha='right', va='bottom', fontsize=9,\n                 bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', facecolor='white', alpha=0.8))\n\n    # 直方图（样本密度）\n    ax_hist.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_counts, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                color=color, alpha=0.5)\n    ax_hist.set_ylabel('Count', fontsize=9)\n    ax_hist.set_xlabel('Confidence')\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n### 批量生成 6 张对比图（EMNLP 论文用）\n\n选图策略：每 Regime 选最大 ΔECE 的量化方法 vs FP16，LLaMA + Qwen3 各一。\n\n```python\n# 在 A6000 服务器上执行\nFIGURE_PLAN = [\n    # (dataset, model, quant, regime, title)\n    ('hellaswag',   'llama',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — LLaMA'),\n    ('hellaswag',   'qwen3',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — Qwen3'),\n    ('arc_c',       'llama',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — LLaMA'),\n    ('arc_c',       'qwen3',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — Qwen3'),\n    ('truthfulqa',  'llama',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — LLaMA'),\n    ('truthfulqa',  'qwen3',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — Qwen3'),\n]\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n\n**用途**：Main Results Table 的可视化版本，颜色 = ΔECE（红=恶化，蓝=改善）。\n\n```python\ndef plot_ece_heatmap(delta_ece_matrix, methods, datasets,\n                     title='ΔECE Heatmap (vs FP16)',\n                     outpath='ece_heatmap.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    delta_ece_matrix: shape (n_methods, n_datasets), 正=ECE 恶化, 负=改善\n    \"\"\"\n    import matplotlib.colors as mcolors\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(len(datasets) * 1.2, len(methods) * 0.8 + 1))\n\n    # 发散色图（中心=0, 红=恶化, 蓝=改善）\n    vmax = np.nanmax(np.abs(delta_ece_matrix))\n    norm = mcolors.TwoSlopeNorm(vmin=-vmax, vcenter=0, vmax=vmax)\n    cmap = plt.cm.RdBu_r\n\n    im = ax.imshow(delta_ece_matrix, cmap=cmap, norm=norm, aspect='auto')\n\n    # 数值标注\n    for i in range(len(methods)):\n        for j in range(len(datasets)):\n            val = delta_ece_matrix[i, j]\n            if not np.isnan(val):\n                color = 'white' if abs(val) > vmax * 0.6 else 'black'\n                sign = '+' if val > 0 else ''\n                ax.text(j, i, f'{sign}{val:.1%}', ha='center', va='center',\n                        fontsize=9, color=color, fontweight='bold')\n\n    ax.set_xticks(range(len(datasets)))\n    ax.set_yticks(range(len(methods)))\n    ax.set_xticklabels(datasets, rotation=30, ha='right')\n    ax.set_yticklabels(methods)\n    ax.set_title(title)\n\n    cbar = plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)\n    cbar.set_label('ΔECE (quantized − FP16)')\n    cbar.ax.axhline(0, color='k', linewidth=0.8)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n\n**用途**：展示 5 个模型的 FP16 T* 分布，证明 Regime 跨模型一致性。\n\n```python\ndef plot_tstar_scatter(tstar_data, outpath='tstar_scatter.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    tstar_data: dict of {model_name: {dataset: T*_value}}\n    \"\"\"\n    DATASETS = ['HellaSwag', 'ARC-C', 'MMLU', 'BoolQ', 'TruthfulQA', 'NQ-Open']\n    MODELS = ['LLaMA-3.1-8B', 'Qwen3-4B', 'Gemma-3-12B', 'Mistral-7B', 'Phi-3-mini']\n    REGIME_BANDS = [\n        (0, 1,    '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A\\n(T*<1)'),\n        (1, 5,    '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B\\n(1≤T*<5)'),\n        (5, 10.5, '#FDF2F8', 'Regime C\\n(T*≥5)'),\n    ]\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 4))\n\n    # 背景色区\n    for lo, hi, color, label in REGIME_BANDS:\n        ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n        ax.text(5.8, (lo + hi) / 2, label, va='center', fontsize=9,\n                color='gray', zorder=1)\n\n    model_colors = [COLORS.get(m.split('-')[0].lower(), '#333333') for m in MODELS]\n\n    x = np.arange(len(DATASETS))\n    for mi, (model, color) in enumerate(zip(MODELS, model_colors)):\n        y = [tstar_data.get(model, {}).get(ds, np.nan) for ds in DATASETS]\n        ax.plot(x, y, 'o-', color=color, label=model,\n                markersize=7, alpha=0.85, zorder=2)\n\n    ax.set_xticks(x)\n    ax.set_xticklabels(DATASETS, rotation=15, ha='right')\n    ax.set_ylabel('T* (FP16)')\n    ax.set_ylim(0, 10.5)\n    ax.set_title('Calibration Regime by Task — 5 Models (FP16)')\n    ax.axhline(1, color='gray', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.axhline(5, color='gray', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9, ncol=2)\n    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.2, axis='y', zorder=1)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. 生成流程（SSH 执行）\n\n```python\n# Step 1: 写脚本到服务器\nssh_execute(\"\"\"cat > /tmp/gen_figures.py << 'PYEOF'\n# ... 粘贴上方代码 + 调用逻辑 ...\nPYEOF\"\"\")\n\n# Step 2: 安装依赖（如有）\nssh_execute(\"pip install matplotlib numpy -q\")\n\n# Step 3: 执行\nssh_execute(\"cd ~/quant-calibration-gap && python /tmp/gen_figures.py\")\n\n# Step 4: PNG → PDF（LaTeX 用 PDF 更清晰）\nssh_execute(\"\"\"\nfor f in ~/quant-calibration-gap/figures/paper/*.png; do\n    convert \"$f\" \"${f%.png}.pdf\" 2>/dev/null || \\\n    python3 -c \"\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.image as mpimg\nimg = mpimg.imread('$f')\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(img.shape[1]/300, img.shape[0]/300))\nax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1])\nax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\nplt.savefig('${f%.png}.pdf', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\nplt.close()\"\ndone\n\"\"\")\n\n# Step 5: 验证\nssh_execute(\"ls ~/quant-calibration-gap/figures/paper/\")\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Pattern 实现（按 design_notes 选用）\n\n完整 pattern 目录和何时用见 `paper-visualization.skill.md` L3。这里给可直接复制的实现。\n\n### Pattern A：Regime Background Bands\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\n# 横向 regime bands\nREGIME_BANDS = [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'),\n                (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B'),\n                (5, 10.5, '#FDF2F8', 'Regime C')]\nfor lo, hi, color, label in REGIME_BANDS:\n    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center',\n            fontsize=9, color='gray', zorder=1)\n\n# 单一 collapse zone\nax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\nax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8,\n        color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic', va='center')\n```\n\n### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n\n```python\n# alpha 极低（0.06）：不抢眼但有面积\nax.fill_between(x_range, curve_upper, curve_lower,\n                alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1, label='_nolegend_')\n```\n\n### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n\n```python\nax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':',\n           linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\nt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value + 1, 'actual W3A16',\n            fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5, ha='center')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid by sample size）\n\n```python\n# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心带 n 标注 = n < 50\nsolid_mask = [n >= 50 for n in n_vals]\nfor x, y, n, solid in zip(xs, ys, n_vals, solid_mask):\n    if solid:\n        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n                markerfacecolor=color, markeredgewidth=0)\n    else:\n        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n                markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n                markeredgewidth=1.2, zorder=4)\n        ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), textcoords='offset points',\n                    xytext=(0, 7), fontsize=5, color=color,\n                    ha='center', fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern E：Wilson Binomial CI\n\n```python\ndef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n    if n == 0: return 0, 0\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n\n# 用法\nlo, hi = wilson_ci(accuracy, n)\nax.errorbar(x, accuracy*100, yerr=[[accuracy*100 - lo*100], [hi*100 - accuracy*100]],\n            fmt='o', capsize=4, capthick=0.8, elinewidth=0.8,\n            markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.0)\n```\n\n### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（防标注重叠）\n\n```python\nlabel_offsets = {\n    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),\n    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),\n    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n}\nfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy),\n                textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7,\n                arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', color='gray',\n                                lw=0.4) if abs(ox) > 20 else None)\n```\n\n### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n\n```python\nfrom matplotlib.patches import Patch\nfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n    if is_collapse:\n        bar.set_hatch('///')\n        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n# Legend 加 hatching 说明\nlegend_patch = Patch(facecolor='gray', hatch='///', alpha=0.7, label='Collapse')\nax.legend(handles=[legend_patch], loc='lower right')\n```\n\n### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n\n```python\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n# axes[0] 是主面板（1.4x 宽），axes[1] 副面板\n# 给每个 panel 写 insight subtitle\naxes[0].set_title('(a) Quantization shifts confidence', fontsize=9, pad=6)\naxes[1].set_title('(b) Temperature scaling recovers it', fontsize=9, pad=6)\n```\n\n### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n\n```python\nd = 0.012  # 斜线长度\nkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black',\n              clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)\n```\n\n### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern K：Concept curve（合成轨迹建立直觉）\n\n```python\n# 完全不基于实验数据的概念曲线\nx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\ntrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\nax.plot(x, trajectory, color='#0072B2', linewidth=2,\n        solid_capstyle='round', label='Theoretical trajectory')\n\n# 关键节点用 insight 标注\nax.annotate('gap eliminated', xy=(1.0, final_y),\n            xytext=(0.7, final_y - 5), color='#388E3C',\n            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9,\n            arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color='#388E3C', lw=0.8))\n```\n\n### Pattern L：Forest Plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n\n```python\n# 3-column GridSpec: label | CI plot | inline stats\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(7, 3.5))\ngs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[0.8, 2, 1.2], wspace=0.1)\nax_label = fig.add_subplot(gs[0]); ax_label.axis('off')\nax_main = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\nax_stat = fig.add_subplot(gs[2]); ax_stat.axis('off')\n\n# 等价带（半透明 axvspan）\nax_main.axvspan(-1, 1, alpha=0.1, color='gray', label='±1pp equivalence')\n\n# 每行：dot + horizontal CI line\nfor i, (name, mean, lo, hi, pval) in enumerate(rows):\n    ax_label.text(0.5, i, name, fontsize=9, va='center', ha='center')\n    ax_main.plot([lo, hi], [i, i], color='black', linewidth=1)\n    ax_main.plot(mean, i, 'D', color='#0072B2', markersize=7)\n    ax_stat.text(0.5, i, f'{mean:+.2f} [{lo:+.2f}, {hi:+.2f}]  p={pval:.3f}',\n                 fontsize=8, va='center', ha='center', family='monospace')\n\nax_main.axvline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.6)\nax_main.invert_yaxis()\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. 图表 Checklist（提交前）\n\n### L1 防丑（底线）\n- [ ] 300 DPI（`savefig.dpi=300`）+ PDF 矢量\n- [ ] 色盲友好配色（Okabe-Ito 或语义色板）\n- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n- [ ] 字号 The real issue is that this figure exists but isn't referenced anywhere in the paper. I need to redesign it with proper colors matching the semantic palette, improve the overall quality through multiple iterations, then integrate it into the LaTeX with a caption that conveys the main finding: each architecture develops a distinct per-example attention profile.

I'm considering a diverging horizontal bar layout to make the net effects more visually striking—architectures on the y-axis with helped examples extending right and hurt examples extending left—though the current vertical grouped bars are more conventional. Either way, I'll apply the paper's color scheme, tighten the typography, and add the Jaccard overlap insight to make the narrative clear.

But the experiments section is already crowded with figures and tables, so I need to weigh whether adding another one creates layout problems or genuinely improves clarity. I'm realizing that §4.1 is the section that would benefit most from a new figure since it currently only has text and a table reference. If I insert a figure there, LaTeX will automatically renumber it as Figure 2, which means I'd need to update all the subsequent figure references throughout the paper—but that's actually the cleanest approach since the numbering will cascade naturally. The references will still work fine since they use labels rather than hard-coded numbers, so adding a new figure won't break anything. I'll improve the existing figure, generate it with the virtual environment, and insert it into section 4.1 with a proper caption and text reference. For the design, I'm thinking about using the paper's color scheme—safe green (#009E73) for helped cases and collapse orange (#D55E00) for hurt cases—along with a subtitle that clarifies the narrative and better typography for a single-column layout. Rather than a basic grouped bar chart, I'm considering a diverging bar approach to make it more visually informative. chart (butterfly)**
- Y-axis: architectures
- Helped extends right, Hurt extends left

**Option 3: Stacked bar with overlap annotation**
- Show helped + hurt stacked
- Add Jaccard values between pairs

I think Option 1 (diverging/butterfly) would be more visually distinctive and immediately communicates the asymmetry. Let me design that.

Actually, thinking more carefully about what makes a good figure for this data:

The key message is:
1. Each architecture has a distinct helped/hurt profile
2. HGRN2 is uniquely beneficial
3. The overlap between architectures is only ~10%

A butterfly chart shows both the helped/hurt asymmetry and the relative differences between architectures clearly. To highlight the low overlap, I could add Jaccard annotations or connecting lines between bars.

For scope, I'll focus on LAMBADA since that's what the text emphasizes first, keeping the figure simpler and more focused. I'll create a clean butterfly chart with the three architectures, color-coded helped and hurt bars, annotated net effects, and the mean pairwise Jaccard value somewhere visible. I should use the paper's semantic colors for consistency.

Now I need to decide whether to polish the existing grouped bar chart or start fresh with the butterfly design. Given the requirements, I think recreating it will give me better control over the aesthetics and alignment with the paper's visual language. Writing the visualization script...

- ✅ Color semantics are solid (green for gains, vermillion for losses)
**L3 (高级感):**
- ✅ Custom styling moves beyond default matplotlib
- ✅ Muted colorblind palette is applied
- ✅ Consistent typography and gridlines
- ⚠️ Still need to evaluate the final polish

: the legend background has a warm tint that clashes slightly with the white plot area, and the legend placement overlaps with the GDN bars. The net difference labels could use visual anchors like connecting lines to make their reference clearer.

For the next iteration, I'm focusing on repositioning the legend to the lower right where there's more breathing room, and refining the subtitle to be more insight-driven rather than just descriptive. The core message is already clear—GDN and RetNet show net hurt while HGRN2 shows net help—so I'll keep the polish minimal and move forward with the LaTeX integration.

16	
17	# LaTeX 论文写作全流程
18	
19	ML/AI 顶会论文从草稿到投稿的完整指南。整合 Orchestra Research、VibeSkills、claim2evidence 三方最佳实践。
20	
21	---
22	
23	## 1. 工作模式
24	
25	开始前选择模式：
26	
27	| 模式 | 说明 | 适用 |
28	|------|------|------|
29	| **Build** | 从实验笔记/结果/代码生成论文 | 新稿 |
30	| **Improve** | 改写已有章节，强化论证 | 修改稿 |
31	| **Audit** | 输出风险列表 + 缺失证据 + 修复建议 | 投前检查 |
32	
33	---
34	
35	## 2. Claim-Evidence 方法论
36	
37	**核心原则：论文是一个有中心贡献的故事，不是实验的堆砌。**
38	
39	### 2.1 一句话贡献
40	
41	在写任何内容之前，必须能用一句话说清贡献。如果不能，说明还没准备好写论文。
42	
43	### 2.2 Claim-Evidence Map
44	
45	为每个 contribution 建立 claim→evidence 映射表：
46	
47	| Contribution | Claim | Evidence | Table/Figure |
48	|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|
49	| C1: 方法 X 在 Y 上 SOTA | 比所有 baseline 高 ≥2% | 5-seed 平均，p<0.05 | Table 1 |
50	| C2: 消融证明 Z 是关键 | 去掉 Z 后掉 >5% | 消融实验 | Table 2 |
51	| C3: 计算效率 | 比 baseline 快 3× | FLOPs + wall-clock | Figure 3 |
52	
53	**没有直接证据支撑的 claim 必须删除或降级为 discussion。**
54	
55	### 2.3 章节起草顺序
56	
57	```
58	1. Introduction + Experiments（先确定 claim 和 evidence）
59	2. Method（评估问题稳定后再写细节）
60	3. Related Work
61	4. Abstract + Title（核心 claim 定型后最后写）
62	```
63	
64	### 2.4 Quality Gates
65	
66	投稿前必须通过：
67	- [ ] 前两段出现显式 novelty 声明
68	- [ ] 每个 contribution 有可验证的 evidence
69	- [ ] Abstract / Intro / Conclusion 的 claim 一致
70	- [ ] Limitations 真实且技术性诚实
71	- [ ] 审稿人两分钟内能识别 delta
72	
73	---
74	
75	## 3. 引用防幻觉（CRITICAL）
76	
77	**AI 生成引用有 ~40% 错误率。绝对禁止凭记忆写 BibTeX。**
78	
79	### 3.1 黄金法则
80	
81	```
82	IF 无法程序化获取引用:
83	    → 标记 [CITATION NEEDED] 或 \cite{PLACEHOLDER_verify}
84	    → 明确告知用户
85	    → 绝不编造看似合理的引用
86	```
87	
88	### 3.2 五步验证流程
89	
90	```
91	1. SEARCH  → Semantic Scholar API 搜索关键词
92	2. VERIFY  → 至少 2 个来源确认论文存在（S2 + arXiv/CrossRef）
93	3. RETRIEVE → 通过 DOI 获取 BibTeX（程序化，非记忆）
94	4. CHECK   → 确认你引用的 claim 确实出自该论文
95	5. ADD     → 加入 .bib 文件
96	```
97	
98	### 3.3 API 速查
99	
100	| API | 用途 | 限制 |
````

```text
33	\subsection{Per-Example Divergence}\label{ssec:per_example}
34	
35	Do architectures converge by improving on the same examples? We compare each architecture's pure-recurrent baseline against its h3-1 hybrid on 5{,}153 LAMBADA examples, classifying each as helped (correct only in hybrid), hurt (correct only in pure), or unchanged. Although the decomposition is binary, we verify below that it captures genuine separation in the continuous confidence space (Cohen's $d = 0.73$--$0.92$ between helped and hurt on LAMBADA; cross-benchmark details in Appendix~\ref{app:cm_cross}). This binarization discards confidence magnitude (a transition from 0.51 to 0.49 is treated identically to one from 0.99 to 0.01), so we supplement it with continuous confidence margin analysis (Appendix~\ref{app:confidence_margin}) and verify that low Jaccard persists under continuous thresholds (Appendix~\ref{app:continuous_jaccard}).
36	
37	Each architecture displays a distinct profile of per-example changes. GDN loses a net 153 examples (462 helped, 615 hurt), RetNet loses 69 (426/495), and HGRN2 gains 153 (548/395; full counts in Appendix~\ref{app:per_example_counts}). HGRN2 is the only architecture for which adding attention yields a net per-example benefit. Pairwise Jaccard similarity is low: mean $\bar{J} \leq 0.10$, indicating ${\sim}90$\% non-overlap. Overlap significantly exceeds the independence baseline ($J_{\text{null}} \approx 0.05$, all $z > 5.9$, $p < .0001$; Appendix~\ref{app:jaccard_permutation}), but normalizing by $J_{\max}$, observed overlap reaches only 6--7\% of the achievable range above chance (Appendix~\ref{app:normalized_es}), confirming that low Jaccard cannot be attributed to set-size constraints.
38	
39	\paragraph{Confidence margin predictor.} Pure-model confidence on the target token predicts which examples will be affected (AUC 0.70--0.74; Appendix~\ref{app:confidence_margin}): rescue thresholds are architecture-invariant (1\% spread) but disruption thresholds vary 47\%, explaining cross-architecture Jaccard divergence (Appendix~\ref{app:prediction_framework}). Calibration differences account for less than half the threshold spread (Appendix~\ref{app:calibration}). The rescue pattern generalizes across three benchmarks ($d = 0.13$--$0.60$; Appendix~\ref{app:cm_cross}); disruption is weaker on reasoning tasks (Appendix~\ref{app:boolq}). This structured disagreement constitutes predictive multiplicity \citep{marx2020predictive} under architectural variation \citep[cf.][]{damour2022underspecification}.
40	
41	\paragraph{Confidence-based routing.} As an illustrative case study, the rescue-threshold asymmetry suggests a practical routing strategy: for each example, select the architecture whose pure-model confidence best predicts successful rescue. On LAMBADA, rescue-threshold routing outperforms majority vote by $+5.2$\,pp (SD $= 0.4$\,pp, paired across 10 splits), extracting 36\% of the oracle gap (oracle = 65.5\%; complementarity decomposition in Appendix~\ref{app:complementarity}). On ARC-Easy, gains are modest ($+1.2$\,pp, SD $= 0.5$\,pp); on HellaSwag, routing provides no significant benefit ($-0.06$\,pp, $p = .374$); on PIQA, it is significantly negative ($-0.49$\,pp, $p = .006$). Using 10-repeat 50/50 cross-validated splits, routing works when multiplicity is high ($\alpha = 0.30$, LAMBADA) and a clear rescue-threshold signal exists, but is counterproductive when multiplicity is low ($\alpha = 0.14$, PIQA).
42	
43	\paragraph{Multiplicity variation.} Extending the per-example analysis to HellaSwag ($N = 10{,}042$) and PIQA ($N = 1{,}838$) reveals that predictive multiplicity varies systematically across tasks (Table~\ref{tab:multiplicity}). The ambiguity $\alpha$ \citep{marx2020predictive} ranges from 0.14 (PIQA) through 0.15 (HellaSwag) and 0.21 (ARC-Easy; Appendix~\ref{app:arceasy}) to 0.30 (LAMBADA), forming a task-dependent variation. Format does not drive multiplicity: HellaSwag (four-way) and PIQA (two-choice) produce nearly identical $\alpha$ despite different formats. Bootstrap 95\% CIs confirm the ordering (all non-overlapping except PIQA--HellaSwag; Appendix, Table~\ref{tab:arceasy_ambiguity}). Moreover, $\alpha$ does not significantly correlate with task accuracy (Spearman $\rho = -0.60$, $p = .24$). Pairwise Jaccard remains uniformly low ($\bar{J} \leq 0.10$, all $p < .01$ across four benchmarks), indicating that architectures affect different examples regardless of how many examples are affected. Within each benchmark, $\alpha$ is stable across attention ratios (variation $< 0.03$), but differs $2\times$ across task types (from reasoning to retrieval), confirming that multiplicity magnitude depends on task, not attention dose \citep[cf.][]{gorbett2026crossmodel}. PIQA (0.14) and HellaSwag (0.15) share a reasoning-task floor, while retrieval (LAMBADA, 0.30) and mixed (ARC-Easy, 0.21) tasks produce higher values.
44	
45	\begin{table}[t]
46	\centering
47	\small
48	\caption{Predictive multiplicity at 1.3B. $\alpha$: ambiguity at h3-1 \citep{marx2020predictive}. $\bar{J}$: mean pairwise Jaccard of helped~(h) and hurt~(u) sets, averaged across four hybrid attention ratios and three architecture pairs. $\rho_\alpha$: Spearman $\rho$ between attention ratio and $\alpha$. Benchmarks ordered by $\alpha$.}
49	\label{tab:multiplicity}
50	\begin{tabular}{@{}lrcccc@{}}
51	\toprule
52	 & $N$ & $\alpha$ & $\bar{J}_{h}$ & $\bar{J}_{u}$ & $\rho_\alpha$ \\

```text
35	Do architectures converge by improving on the same examples? We compare each architecture's pure-recurrent baseline against its h3-1 hybrid on 5{,}153 LAMBADA examples, classifying each as helped (correct only in hybrid), hurt (correct only in pure), or unchanged. Although the decomposition is binary, we verify below that it captures genuine separation in the continuous confidence space (Cohen's $d = 0.73$--$0.92$ between helped and hurt on LAMBADA; cross-benchmark details in Appendix~\ref{app:cm_cross}). This binarization discards confidence magnitude (a transition from 0.51 to 0.49 is treated identically to one from 0.99 to 0.01), so we supplement it with continuous confidence margin analysis (Appendix~\ref{app:confidence_margin}) and verify that low Jaccard persists under continuous thresholds (Appendix~\ref{app:continuous_jaccard}).
36	
37	Each architecture displays a distinct profile of per-example changes (Figure~\ref{fig:per_example_bars}). GDN loses a net 153 examples (462 helped, 615 hurt), RetNet loses 69 (426/495), and HGRN2 gains 153 (548/395; full counts in Appendix~\ref{app:per_example_counts}). HGRN2 is the only architecture for which adding attention yields a net per-example benefit. Pairwise Jaccard similarity is low: mean $\bar{J} \leq 0.10$, indicating ${\sim}90$\% non-overlap. Overlap significantly exceeds the independence baseline ($J_{\text{null}} \approx 0.05$, all $z > 5.9$, $p < .0001$; Appendix~\ref{app:jaccard_permutation}), but normalizing by $J_{\max}$, observed overlap reaches only 6--7\% of the achievable range above chance (Appendix~\ref{app:normalized_es}), confirming that low Jaccard cannot be attributed to set-size constraints.
38	
39	\begin{figure}[t]
40	\centering
41	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig_per_example_bars.pdf}
42	\caption{Per-example effects of adding attention (pure $\to$ h3-1) on LAMBADA at 1.3B scale. Helped: correct only in the hybrid; hurt: correct only in the pure model. HGRN2 is the only architecture with a net positive effect. Despite comparable magnitudes, these sets overlap minimally across architectures ($\bar{J} \leq 0.10$, all $p < .0001$).}
43	\label{fig:per_example_bars}
44	\end{figure}
45	
46	\paragraph{Confidence margin predictor.} Pure-model confidence on the target token predicts which examples will be affected (AUC 0.70--0.74; Appendix~\ref{app:confidence_margin}): rescue thresholds are architecture-invariant (1\% spread) but disruption thresholds vary 47\%, explaining cross-architecture Jaccard divergence (Appendix~\ref{app:prediction_framework}). Calibration differences account for less than half the threshold spread (Appendix~\ref{app:calibration}). The rescue pattern generalizes across three benchmarks ($d = 0.13$--$0.60$; Appendix~\ref{app:cm_cross}); disruption is weaker on reasoning tasks (Appendix~\ref{app:boolq}). This structured disagreement constitutes predictive multiplicity \citep{marx2020predictive} under architectural variation \citep[cf.][]{damour2022underspecification}.
47	
48	\paragraph{Confidence-based routing.} As an illustrative case study, the rescue-threshold asymmetry suggests a practical routing strategy: for each example, select the architecture whose pure-model confidence best predicts successful rescue. On LAMBADA, rescue-threshold routing outperforms majority vote by $+5.2$\,pp (SD $= 0.4$\,pp, paired across 10 splits), extracting 36\% of the oracle gap (oracle = 65.5\%; complementarity decomposition in Appendix~\ref{app:complementarity}). On ARC-Easy, gains are modest ($+1.2$\,pp, SD $= 0.5$\,pp); on HellaSwag, routing provides no significant benefit ($-0.06$\,pp, $p = .374$); on PIQA, it is significantly negative ($-0.49$\,pp, $p = .006$). Using 10-repeat 50/50 cross-validated splits, routing works when multiplicity is high ($\alpha = 0.30$, LAMBADA) and a clear rescue-threshold signal exists, but is counterproductive when multiplicity is low ($\alpha = 0.14$, PIQA).
49	
50	\paragraph{Multiplicity variation.} Extending the per-example analysis to HellaSwag ($N = 10{,}042$) and PIQA ($N = 1{,}838$) reveals that predictive multiplicity varies systematically across tasks (Table~\ref{tab:multiplicity}). The ambiguity $\alpha$ \citep{marx2020predictive} ranges from 0.14 (PIQA) through 0.15 (HellaSwag) and 0.21 (ARC-Easy; Appendix~\ref{app:arceasy}) to 0.30 (LAMBADA), forming a task-dependent variation. Format does not drive multiplicity: HellaSwag (four-way) and PIQA (two-choice) produce nearly identical $\alpha$ despite different formats. Bootstrap 95\% CIs confirm the ordering (all non-overlapping except PIQA--HellaSwag; Appendix, Table~\ref{tab:arceasy_ambiguity}). Moreover, $\alpha$ does not significantly correlate with task accuracy (Spearman $\rho = -0.60$, $p = .24$). Pairwise Jaccard remains uniformly low ($\bar{J} \leq 0.10$, all $p < .01$ across four benchmarks), indicating that architectures affect different examples regardless of how many examples are affected. Within each benchmark, $\alpha$ is stable across attention ratios (variation $< 0.03$), but differs $2\times$ across task types (from reasoning to retrieval), confirming that multiplicity magnitude depends on task, not attention dose \citep[cf.][]{gorbett2026crossmodel}. PIQA (0.14) and HellaSwag (0.15) share a reasoning-task floor, while retrieval (LAMBADA, 0.30) and mixed (ARC-Easy, 0.21) tasks produce higher values.
51	
52	\begin{table}[t]
53	\centering
54	\small

18	
19	# 论文写作风格规范
20	
21	> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。
22	
23	---
24	
25	## 一、总体写作哲学
26	
27	### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌
28	
29	- 论文必须以"故事线"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证
30	- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进
31	- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌
32	- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing
33	
34	### 1.2 主线统一性
35	
36	- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**
37	- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C
38	- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机
39	- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线
40	
41	### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义
42	
43	- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释
44	- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）
45	- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用
46	- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug
47	
48	### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文
49	
50	- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可
51	- 重点学：
52	  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事
53	  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献
54	  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）
55	  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度
56	- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大
57	- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文
58	
59	---
60	
61	## 二、Abstract 写作规范
62	
63	### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）
64	
65	Abstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：
66	1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点
67	2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）
68	3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）
69	4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献
70	
71	### 2.2 禁忌
72	
73	- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim
74	- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 "revolutionary"、"groundbreaking"
75	- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元
76	- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了
77	- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开
78	
79	### 2.3 审稿人视角检验
80	
```
